Both things are done — the pin, and I've read the project.

CONFIG PIN — fixed
- `~/.hermes/config.yaml` had `model.context_length: 1048576`, and the startup warning was: pin at 1,048,576 vs provider advertising 1,000,000 → the pin wins, and 1,048,576 is over the real window, so any long session would have failed on the way into it.
- `hermes config unset model.context_length` — pin gone. The provider's advertised 1,000,000 (1M) now governs; no pin, no warning, no overshoot. Takes effect on the next gateway start (this session booted with the old pin).
- (The patch/write tools refuse config.yaml by design; `hermes config` is the sanctioned path.)

HYDRA V2 — I understand it
Working copy: \\192.168.1.100\receive\hydraV2 (old V1 network still live at \\192.168.1.100\receive\deepseek\app\Hydra, server :8111).

agent
It's in the app now. One window, four tabs: **Basic | YuE studio | LLM chat | Process** — the studio page is no longer a browser thing.

What I changed to get there
- Copied Electron_Wrapper locally and used Basic_App's own shell (it already wraps the shelf in one window; nothing external, no second app).
- Added `Basic_App\electron\tabs.json` — a declared list of extra tabs read at boot, so a service that owns a page gets a tab instead of a browser. `extraTabs()` in main.cjs inserts them between Basic and Process.
- Changed yue-music's gui from `kind:'url'` (which ran `start "" <url>` → system browser) to `kind:'command'` → Follow gui now *starts the studio* in the app's own world. It launches as tag `basic-gui`, so it does not lock the shelf as a "run".

Verified, with the window open
- YuE studio tab, live: "YUE STUDIO — READY · node 123 · YuE2-3B on RX 6800 · one song at a time, ~15-90 min", style box, lyrics with markers, seed 1985, mode/budget, and the songs list (subpoena…, the-world-is-my-board-game…, studio-smoke…) with players. Screenshot: C:\Users\User\AppData\Local\hermes\cache\scratch\cdp-window.png
- Process tab: tabs ProcessWrapper | yue-music:gui | qwen-image-2.1 (ended) | sd-probe (ended) | system-watch, with live stream output and the "add Process Console Display" bar. That's "all the background shells" in one pane. Screenshot: C:\Users\User\AppData\Local\hermes\cache\scratch\cdp-process.png
- Cross-checked from the outside: CDP target list shows the wrapper page plus one iframe per tab — 8130, 8090, 8080, 8125/window.html. The studio really is loaded inside the window, not just present as a label.

Two things you should know
1. Closing that window fires PW `/api/stop` — stop-all, by design. With a song rendering, closing the window kills it. That's the open "Quit means stop" item already recorded in the plan; I did not change it.
2. The LLM chat tab currently shows ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED because llama-chat isn't started. Start it from the shelf and the tab fills; say if you'd rather I drop that tab from tabs.json until it's wanted.

To bring it up yourself: `C:\AI\hydra-local\start-hydra.cmd` then `C:\AI\hydra-local\open-hydra-window.cmd`. Catalog still 4/4 clean, 0 errors, 0 warnings.

I deliberately did not restart the window to pick that up: killing electron can trip the close law (`/api/stop`) and take your running YuE studio with it. Not worth a tray icon mid-session.

What the second page is now
- `yue-music` v0.2.0: the studio page rebuilt *inside the service definition* (`pages.inputs`), same dark studio look and header, and the real fields sit under it — Style (prefilled with your 1980s hard rock prompt), Lyrics, Seed 1985, Mode full/melody/off, Title, VRAM ceiling.
- The old `:8090` server is stopped and dead (connection refused), and I removed its tab from `tabs.json` — the running window still shows it because tabs are read at boot, so it goes away next launch.
- One run = one song, then the process exits. No server in the middle: `yue-song.ps1` writes the request JSON, runs `python -m yue2.cli generate --model C:\AI\hf\YuE2-3B --vae C:\AI\hf\yue-vae --budget 16 --offload-ar`, then copies `audio.flac` to `Desktop\make_music\songs\<title>__YuE2-3B.flac`.

Verified, not assumed
- Field plumbing through the shelf's own path (PW parseCommand → PowerShell params → request JSON): style, mult-line lyrics with `[Verse]`/`[Chorus]`, seed, mode, budget, title all arrived intact — a dry run produced exactly `{"id":…,"style":…,"lyrics":"[Verse]\nA red light…","cot":"full","seed":42}` and exited 0 without touching the model.
- Catalog: 4/4 clean, 0 errors, 0 warnings.
- Screenshot of the new second page inside the window: C:\Users\User\AppData\Local\hermes\cache\scratch\yue-page2.png

Not proven yet: an actual song through the shelf — that's ~90 minutes on the card, and nothing else can use the GPU meanwhile. Say go and I'll fire one with a short lyric set so it lands in ~15-20 minutes rather than 90.

One thing to decide: the page says one run at a time and the shelf enforces it (busy), which is right for the GPU but means you can't queue two takes. If you want "queue three takes overnight", that's the Scheduler talk, not this page.
